def Luce.predictableChance : {n : ℕ} → Luce.Weights n → Equiv.Perm (Fin n) → Fin n → ℝ :=
fun {n} w π k => w.choice (Luce.remaining π k) k
theorem Luce.predictableChance_formula : ∀ {n : ℕ} (w : Luce.Weights n) (π : Equiv.Perm (Fin n)) (k : Fin n),
  Luce.predictableChance w π k = (if k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) k then w.rate k else 0) / w.total (Luce.remaining π k) :=
fun {n} w π k =>
  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans
      (congr
        (congrArg Eq
          (ite.congr_simp (k ∈ Luce.remaining π k) (k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) k)
            (Eq.trans Finset.mem_filter._simp_1
              (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg And (Finset.mem_univ._simp_1 k)) (k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) k))
                (true_and (k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) k))))
            (w.rate k / w.total (Luce.remaining π k)) (w.rate k / w.total {i | k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) i})
            (id (Eq.refl (w.rate k / w.total {i | k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) i}))) 0 0 (Eq.refl 0)))
        (Eq.trans (ite_div (k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) k) (w.rate k) 0 (w.total {i | k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) i}))
          (ite_congr (Eq.refl (k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) k)) (fun a => Eq.refl (w.rate k / w.total {i | k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) i}))
            fun a => zero_div (w.total {i | k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) i}))))
      (eq_self (if k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) k then w.rate k / w.total {i | k ≤ (Equiv.symm π) i} else 0)))
structure Luce.BernoulliProcess.{u_1} {Ω : Type u_1} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω} (μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω) : Type u_1
number of parameters: 3
fields:
  Luce.BernoulliProcess.filtration : MeasureTheory.Filtration ℕ mΩ
  Luce.BernoulliProcess.observation : ℕ → Ω → ℝ
  Luce.BernoulliProcess.probability : ℕ → Ω → ℝ
  Luce.BernoulliProcess.adapted : ∀ (k : ℕ), MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable (self.observation k)
  Luce.BernoulliProcess.predictable : ∀ (k : ℕ), MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable (self.probability k)
  Luce.BernoulliProcess.zero_one : ∀ (k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), self.observation k ω = 0 ∨ self.observation k ω = 1
  Luce.BernoulliProcess.probability_nonneg : ∀ (k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), 0 ≤ self.probability k ω
  Luce.BernoulliProcess.probability_le_one : ∀ (k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), self.probability k ω ≤ 1
  Luce.BernoulliProcess.conditional_mean : ∀ (k : ℕ),
      μ[self.observation k | ↑self.filtration k] =ᵐ[μ] self.probability k
constructor:
  Luce.BernoulliProcess.mk.{u_1} {Ω : Type u_1} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω} {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω}
    (filtration : MeasureTheory.Filtration ℕ mΩ) (observation probability : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)
    (adapted : ∀ (k : ℕ), MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable (observation k))
    (predictable : ∀ (k : ℕ), MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable (probability k))
    (zero_one : ∀ (k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), observation k ω = 0 ∨ observation k ω = 1)
    (probability_nonneg : ∀ (k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), 0 ≤ probability k ω)
    (probability_le_one : ∀ (k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), probability k ω ≤ 1)
    (conditional_mean : ∀ (k : ℕ), μ[observation k | ↑filtration k] =ᵐ[μ] probability k) : Luce.BernoulliProcess μ
def Luce.stoppedMass : (ℕ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℕ → ℝ :=
fun p δ K x => Nat.brecOn x (Luce.stoppedMass._f p δ K)
def Luce.keepTerm : (ℕ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℕ → Prop :=
fun p δ K k => p k ≤ δ ∧ Luce.stoppedMass p δ K k + p k ≤ K
def Luce.stoppedProbability : (ℕ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℕ → ℝ :=
fun p δ K k => if Luce.keepTerm p δ K k then p k else 0
theorem Luce.deletion_subset : ∀ {p : ℕ → ℝ} {δ K : ℝ} {N : ℕ},
  (∀ (k : ℕ), 0 ≤ p k) → (∃ k < N, ¬Luce.keepTerm p δ K k) → (∃ k < N, δ < p k) ∨ K < ∑ j ∈ Finset.range N, p j :=
fun {p} {δ K} {N} hp hdelete =>
  Decidable.byContradiction fun h =>
    Exists.casesOn hdelete fun k h_1 =>
      And.casesOn h_1 fun hk hbad =>
        hbad
          (Luce.keepTerm_of_total_le hp
            (Eq.mp
                (Eq.trans not_or._simp_2
                  (congr
                    (congrArg And
                      (Eq.trans (Mathlib.Tactic.Push.not_exists._simp_1 fun k => k < N ∧ δ < p k)
                        (forall_congr fun k =>
                          Eq.trans (Mathlib.Tactic.Push.not_and_eq (k < N) (δ < p k))
                            (implies_congr (Eq.refl (k < N)) not_lt._simp_1))))
                    not_lt._simp_1))
                h).left
            (Eq.mp
                (Eq.trans not_or._simp_2
                  (congr
                    (congrArg And
                      (Eq.trans (Mathlib.Tactic.Push.not_exists._simp_1 fun k => k < N ∧ δ < p k)
                        (forall_congr fun k =>
                          Eq.trans (Mathlib.Tactic.Push.not_and_eq (k < N) (δ < p k))
                            (implies_congr (Eq.refl (k < N)) not_lt._simp_1))))
                    not_lt._simp_1))
                h).right
            hk)
def Luce.BernoulliProcess.stop.{u_1} : {Ω : Type u_1} →
  {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω} →
    {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω} →
      [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] → Luce.BernoulliProcess μ → ℝ → ℝ → Luce.BernoulliProcess μ :=
fun {Ω} {mΩ} {μ} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] X δ K =>
  { filtration := X.filtration,
    observation := fun k => {ω | Luce.keepTerm (fun j => X.probability j ω) δ K k}.indicator (X.observation k),
    probability := fun k ω => Luce.stoppedProbability (fun j => X.probability j ω) δ K k, adapted := ⋯,
    predictable := ⋯, zero_one := ⋯, probability_nonneg := ⋯, probability_le_one := ⋯, conditional_mean := ⋯ }
theorem Luce.condExp_bernoulli_laplace.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω} {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω}
  [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {m : MeasurableSpace Ω},
  m ≤ mΩ →
    ∀ {I p : Ω → ℝ},
      MeasureTheory.Integrable I μ →
        μ[I | m] =ᵐ[μ] p →
          (∀ᵐ (ω : Ω) ∂μ, I ω = 0 ∨ I ω = 1) →
            ∀ (g : ℝ), μ[fun ω => Real.exp (-g * I ω) | m] =ᵐ[μ] fun ω => 1 - (1 - Real.exp (-g)) * p ω :=
fun {Ω} {mΩ} {μ} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {m} hm {I p} hI hIp h01 g =>
  have heq := Filter.Eventually.mono h01 fun x h => Luce.exp_neg_mul_of_zero_one h;
  have hmul := MeasureTheory.condExp_smul (1 - Real.exp (-g)) I m;
  have hsub :=
    MeasureTheory.condExp_sub (MeasureTheory.integrable_const 1)
      (MeasureTheory.Integrable.const_mul hI (1 - Real.exp (-g))) m;
  Filter.EventuallyEq.trans (MeasureTheory.condExp_congr_ae heq)
    (Filter.mp_mem hIp
      (Filter.mp_mem hmul
        (Filter.mp_mem hsub
          (Filter.univ_mem'
            (id fun ω hs hm' hp =>
              Eq.mpr
                (id
                  (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 1 - (1 - Real.exp (-g)) * p ω)
                    (Eq.mp
                      (congrArg (Eq (μ[fun i => 1 - (1 - Real.exp (-g)) * I i | m] ω))
                        (congrFun'
                          (congrFun'
                            (congrArg HSub.hSub
                              ((fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 c [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure x_1] =>
                                  (fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => MeasureTheory.condExp_const hm) x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 c)
                                ℝ μ Real.normedAddCommGroup RCLike.toInnerProductSpaceReal.toNormedSpace 1))
                            μ[fun x => (1 - Real.exp (-g)) * I x | m])
                          ω))
                      hs)))
                (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => 1 - _a = 1 - (1 - Real.exp (-g)) * p ω) hm'))
                  (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => 1 - (1 - Real.exp (-g)) * _a = 1 - (1 - Real.exp (-g)) * p ω) hp))
                    (Eq.refl (1 - (1 - Real.exp (-g)) * p ω)))))))))
theorem Luce.condExp_normalized_bernoulli.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
  {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {m : MeasurableSpace Ω},
  m ≤ mΩ →
    ∀ {I p : Ω → ℝ},
      MeasureTheory.Integrable I μ →
        μ[I | m] =ᵐ[μ] p →
          (∀ᵐ (ω : Ω) ∂μ, I ω = 0 ∨ I ω = 1) →
            MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable p →
              ∀ (g : ℝ),
                (∀ᵐ (ω : Ω) ∂μ, 1 - p ω * (1 - Real.exp (-g)) ≠ 0) →
                  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun ω => Real.exp (-g * I ω) / (1 - p ω * (1 - Real.exp (-g)))) μ →
                    μ[fun ω => Real.exp (-g * I ω) / (1 - p ω * (1 - Real.exp (-g))) | m] =ᵐ[μ] fun x => 1 :=
fun {Ω} {mΩ} {μ} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {m} hm {I p} hI hIp h01 hp g hd hint =>
  have hN :=
    MeasureTheory.Integrable.congr
      (MeasureTheory.Integrable.sub (MeasureTheory.integrable_const 1)
        (MeasureTheory.Integrable.const_mul hI (1 - Real.exp (-g))))
      (Filter.Eventually.mono h01 fun ω h => Eq.symm (Luce.exp_neg_mul_of_zero_one h));
  have hden :=
    MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable.inv₀
      (MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable.sub MeasureTheory.stronglyMeasurable_const
        (MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable.mul_const hp (1 - Real.exp (-g))));
  have hint' :=
    id
      (Eq.mp
        (congrFun'
          (congrArg
            (@MeasureTheory.Integrable ℝ PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace
              SeminormedAddGroup.toContinuousENorm Ω mΩ)
            (funext fun ω => div_eq_mul_inv (Real.exp (-g * I ω)) (1 - p ω * (1 - Real.exp (-g)))))
          μ)
        hint);
  have h := MeasureTheory.condExp_mul_of_stronglyMeasurable_right hden hint' hN;
  Filter.mp_mem hd
    (Filter.mp_mem (Luce.condExp_bernoulli_laplace hm hI hIp h01 g)
      (Filter.mp_mem
        (Eq.mp
          (congr
            (congrArg (MeasureTheory.ae μ).EventuallyEq
              (congrArg (MeasureTheory.condExp m μ)
                (funext fun i =>
                  Luce.condExp_normalized_bernoulli._simp_1_1 (Real.exp (-g * I i)) (1 - p i * (1 - Real.exp (-g))))))
            (funext fun i =>
              Luce.condExp_normalized_bernoulli._simp_1_1 (μ[fun ω => Real.exp (-g * I ω) | m] i)
                (1 - p i * (1 - Real.exp (-g)))))
          h)
        (Filter.univ_mem'
          (id fun ω hc he hd' =>
            Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 1) hc))
              (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a / (1 - p ω * (1 - Real.exp (-g))) = 1) he))
                (Eq.mpr
                  (eq_of_heq
                    ((fun α a a' e'_2 a_1 =>
                        Eq.casesOn (motive := fun a_2 x => a' = a_2 → e'_2 ≍ x → (a = a_1) ≍ (a' = a_1)) e'_2
                          (fun h =>
                            Eq.ndrec (motive := fun a' => ∀ (e_2 : a = a'), e_2 ≍ Eq.refl a → (a = a_1) ≍ (a' = a_1))
                              (fun e_2 h => HEq.refl (a = a_1)) (Eq.symm h) e'_2)
                          (Eq.refl a') (HEq.refl e'_2))
                      ℝ ((1 - (1 - Real.exp (-g)) * p ω) / (1 - p ω * (1 - Real.exp (-g))))
                      ((1 - p ω * (1 - Real.exp (-g))) / (1 - p ω * (1 - Real.exp (-g))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.div_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.exp (-g)) rfl
                                  (Eq.mpr
                                    (id
                                      (congrArg
                                        (fun _a =>
                                          Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                            Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                        (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                    (Eq.refl (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (-g)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                          (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                                    Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                      (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (p ω) rfl
                                (Eq.mpr
                                  (id
                                    (congrArg (fun _a => p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                      (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                  (Eq.refl (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (p ω) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                        (Eq.refl 1))))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                    (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (Real.exp (-g)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (p ω) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                            (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero
                                      (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                      (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast) +
                                        0)))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                    (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast) +
                                      0)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                                  (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                    (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (-g)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (p ω) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                          (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 1)))))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add

                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                        (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                                  Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)

                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                    (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          ((Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                                  (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                                      (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                    (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))⁻¹ ^
                                              Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                            Nat.rawCast 1)) +
                                      0)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (p ω) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                        (Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                            (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                              (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))⁻¹
                                        (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                            (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero
                                      (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                      (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          ((Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                                  (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                                      (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                    (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))⁻¹ ^
                                              Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                            (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast) +
                                        0)))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                    ((Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                              (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))⁻¹ ^
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                      0))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                    (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        ((Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                                (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                  (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))⁻¹ ^
                                            Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast) +
                                      0)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                                  (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      ((Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                              (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))⁻¹ ^
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        Nat.rawCast 1)))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                    (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        ((Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                                (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                  (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))⁻¹ ^
                                            Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast) +
                                      0))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                                (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    ((Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                            (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                              (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))⁻¹ ^
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      Nat.rawCast 1)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                                  (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    ((Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                            (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                              (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))⁻¹ ^
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                    ((Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                              (Real.exp (-g) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                (p ω ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))⁻¹ ^
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                      0))))))))
                      1))
                  (div_self hd')))))))
theorem Luce.bernoulli_likelihood_second_moment_le : ∀ {p q δ : ℝ},
  0 ≤ p →
    p ≤ δ →
      δ < 1 →
        0 ≤ q →
          q ≤ 1 → (1 - p) * (1 / (1 - p * q)) ^ 2 + p * ((1 - q) / (1 - p * q)) ^ 2 ≤ Real.exp (p / (1 - δ) ^ 2) :=
fun {p q δ} hp hpδ hδ hq hq1 =>
  have hd := Luce.likelihood_denominator_pos hp hpδ hδ hq1;
  Eq.mpr
    (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ Real.exp (p / (1 - δ) ^ 2)) (Luce.bernoulli_likelihood_second_moment (LT.lt.ne' hd))))
    (have hδ0 := LE.le.trans hp hpδ;
    have hdδ := sub_pos.mpr hδ;
    have hp1 := LE.le.trans hpδ (LT.lt.le hδ);
    have hq2 :=
      le_of_not_gt fun a =>
        Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
          (Eq.mp
            (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf q rfl
                        (Eq.mpr
                          (id
                            (congrArg (fun _a => q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                              (Eq.symm rfl)))
                          (Eq.refl (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                              (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.pow_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf q rfl
                          (Eq.mpr
                            (id
                              (congrArg (fun _a => q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                (Eq.symm rfl)))
                            (Eq.refl (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℕ (Eq.refl 2)))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.pow_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.single_pow
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pow_mul
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℕ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℕ 2)
                                  (Eq.refl 2)))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.one_pow (Nat.rawCast 2) { out := rfl })
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg (fun _a => q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * Nat.rawCast 1 = q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * Nat.rawCast 1)))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left q (Nat.rawCast 2)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                    (Eq.refl 1))))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.pow_zero (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0) rfl)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left q (Nat.rawCast 2)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                    (Eq.refl 1))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul (Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul q (Nat.rawCast 2)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                            (q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                          (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                          (q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf q rfl
                          (Eq.mpr
                            (id
                              (congrArg (fun _a => q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                (Eq.symm rfl)))
                            (Eq.refl (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul q (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                            Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                            (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf q rfl
                          (Eq.mpr
                            (id
                              (congrArg (fun _a => q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                (Eq.symm rfl)))
                            (Eq.refl (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))
                            Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left q (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 1))))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pp_pf_overlap q
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℕ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℕ 1)
                                  (Eq.refl 2)))
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                              (q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                              (q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                          ((Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                          (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + (q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul q (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                              (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul q (Nat.rawCast 2)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 1))))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                        (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + (q ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero q (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero q (Nat.rawCast 2)
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                            (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_neg_of_le
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_le_of_neg (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le hq1)
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le
                (mul_nonneg_of_nonpos_of_nonpos (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le hq)
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le hq1)))));
    have hn :=
      Trans.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hq2 (mul_nonneg hp (sub_nonneg.mpr hp1)))
        (le_of_not_gt fun a =>
          Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
            (Eq.mp
              (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf p rfl
                        (Eq.mpr
                          (id
                            (congrArg (fun _a => p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                              (Eq.symm rfl)))
                          (Eq.refl (p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf p rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg (fun _a => p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf p rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg (fun _a => p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul p (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                      (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                                Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                  (p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left p (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                    (Eq.refl 1))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pp_pf_overlap p
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℕ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℕ 1)
                                      (Eq.refl 2)))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                      (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                  (p ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                  (p ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
