                                    (p ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                    (p ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                (Nat.rawCast 1 + (p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                (p ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                  (p ^ Nat.rawCast 2 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.pow_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf q rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg (fun _a => q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (q ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℕ (Eq.refl 2)))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.pow_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.single_pow
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pow_mul
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℕ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℕ 2)
                                      (Eq.refl 2)))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.one_pow (Nat.rawCast 2) { out := rfl }) (Eq.mpr (id ⋯) ⋯)
                                  ⋯))
                              ⋯ ⋯))
                          ⋯)
                        ⋯ ⋯)
                      ⋯)
                    ⋯ ⋯)
                  ⋯ ⋯)
                ⋯ ⋯)
              ⋯))
          ⋯))
def Luce.BernoulliProcess.likelihood.{u_1} : {Ω : Type u_1} →
  {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω} → {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω} → Luce.BernoulliProcess μ → (ℕ → ℝ) → ℕ → Ω → ℝ :=
fun {Ω} {mΩ} {μ} X g N ω => ∏ k ∈ Finset.range N, X.laplaceFactor (g k) k ω
theorem Luce.BernoulliProcess.likelihood_martingale.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
  {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (X : Luce.BernoulliProcess μ) {δ : ℝ},
  δ < 1 →
    ∀ (g : ℕ → ℝ),
      (∀ (k : ℕ), 0 ≤ g k) →
        (∀ (k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), X.probability k ω ≤ δ) → MeasureTheory.Martingale (X.likelihood g) X.filtration μ :=
fun {Ω} {mΩ} {μ} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] X {δ} hδ g hg hpδ =>
  Luce.likelihood_product_martingale X.filtration (fun k => X.laplaceFactor (g k) k)
    (fun k => Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceFactor_stronglyMeasurable X (g k) k)
    (fun k => Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceFactor_integrable X hδ (hg k) k (hpδ k))
    (fun k => Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceFactor_conditional_mean X hδ (hg k) k (hpδ k))
    (Luce.BernoulliProcess.likelihood_integrable X hδ g hg hpδ)
theorem Luce.BernoulliProcess.integral_likelihood.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
  {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (X : Luce.BernoulliProcess μ) {δ : ℝ},
  δ < 1 →
    ∀ (g : ℕ → ℝ),
      (∀ (k : ℕ), 0 ≤ g k) →
        (∀ (k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), X.probability k ω ≤ δ) → ∀ (N : ℕ), ∫ (ω : Ω), X.likelihood g N ω ∂μ = 1 :=
fun {Ω} {mΩ} {μ} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] X {δ} hδ g hg hpδ N =>
  Luce.likelihood_product_integral X.filtration (fun k => X.laplaceFactor (g k) k)
    (fun k => Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceFactor_stronglyMeasurable X (g k) k)
    (fun k => Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceFactor_integrable X hδ (hg k) k (hpδ k))
    (fun k => Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceFactor_conditional_mean X hδ (hg k) k (hpδ k))
    (Luce.BernoulliProcess.likelihood_integrable X hδ g hg hpδ) N
theorem Luce.BernoulliProcess.likelihood_bounds_of_sum_le.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
  {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (X : Luce.BernoulliProcess μ) {δ K : ℝ},
  δ < 1 →
    ∀ (g : ℕ → ℝ),
      (∀ (k : ℕ), 0 ≤ g k) →
        (∀ (k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), X.probability k ω ≤ δ) →
          ∀ (N : ℕ) (ω : Ω),
            ∑ k ∈ Finset.range N, X.probability k ω ≤ K →
              0 ≤ X.likelihood g N ω ∧ X.likelihood g N ω ≤ Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) :=
fun {Ω} {mΩ} {μ} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] X {δ K} hδ g hg hpδ N ω hK =>
  let x := fun k => X.probability k ω * (1 - Real.exp (-g k));
  have hx := fun k =>
    mul_nonneg (X.probability_nonneg k ω) (sub_nonneg.mpr (Real.exp_le_one_iff.mpr (neg_nonpos.mpr (hg k))));
  have hxp := fun k =>
    mul_le_of_le_one_right (X.probability_nonneg k ω)
      (le_of_not_gt fun a =>
        Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
          (Eq.mp
            (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.exp (-g k)) rfl
                        (Eq.mpr
                          (id
                            (congrArg
                              (fun _a =>
                                Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                              (Eq.symm rfl)))
                          (Eq.refl (Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (-g k)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                            (Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                            (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (-g k)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 1))))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                          (Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.exp (-g k)) rfl
                      (Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg
                            (fun _a =>
                              Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                            (Eq.symm rfl)))
                        (Eq.refl (Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (-g k)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                              (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                        (Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (Real.exp (-g k)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0)))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_neg (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a)
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt (Real.exp_pos (-g k))))));
  have hsum := LE.le.trans (Finset.sum_le_sum fun k x => hxp k) hK;
  have hinv :=
    Luce.inverse_product_le_exp (Finset.range N) x (fun k x => hx k) (fun k x_1 => LE.le.trans (hxp k) (hpδ k ω)) hδ
      hsum;
  have hp := (Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceProduct_bounds X hδ g hg hpδ N ω).left;
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg (fun _a => 0 ≤ _a ∧ _a ≤ Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)))
        (Luce.BernoulliProcess.likelihood_eq_laplace_div X g N ω)))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg (fun _a => 0 ≤ _a ∧ _a ≤ Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)))
          (div_eq_mul_inv (Real.exp (-∑ k ∈ Finset.range N, g k * X.observation k ω)) (X.laplaceProduct g N ω))))
      ⟨mul_nonneg (LT.lt.le (Real.exp_pos (-∑ k ∈ Finset.range N, g k * X.observation k ω)))
          (LT.lt.le (inv_pos.mpr hp)),
        Trans.trans
          (mul_le_of_le_one_left (LT.lt.le (inv_pos.mpr hp))
            (Real.exp_le_one_iff.mpr
              (neg_nonpos.mpr
                (Finset.sum_nonneg fun k x => mul_nonneg (hg k) (Luce.BernoulliProcess.observation_nonneg X k ω)))))
          hinv⟩)
theorem Luce.product_poisson_error_of_atom_bound.{u_1} : ∀ {ι : Type u_1} (s : Finset ι) (x : ι → ℝ) {a δ K lam : ℝ},
  (∀ i ∈ s, 0 ≤ x i) →
    (∀ i ∈ s, x i ≤ a) →
      0 ≤ a →
        a ≤ δ →
          δ < 1 →
            ∑ i ∈ s, x i ≤ K →
              0 ≤ lam → |∏ i ∈ s, (1 - x i) - Real.exp (-lam)| ≤ K / (1 - δ) * a + |∑ i ∈ s, x i - lam| :=
fun {ι} s x {a δ K lam} hx hxa ha haδ hδ hsum hlam =>
  have hsum0 := Finset.sum_nonneg hx;
  have hK := LE.le.trans hsum0 hsum;
  LE.le.trans (Luce.product_poisson_error s x hx hxa (LE.le.trans_lt haδ hδ) hlam)
    (add_le_add
      (Trans.trans
        (Trans.trans
          (div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hsum ha)
            (LT.lt.le (sub_pos.mpr (LE.le.trans_lt haδ hδ))))
          (div_le_div_of_nonneg_left (mul_nonneg ha hK) (sub_pos.mpr hδ)
            (le_of_not_gt fun a_1 =>
              Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
                (Eq.mp
                  (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf a rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg (fun _a => a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf δ rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg (fun _a => δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul δ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                    (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                              Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf a rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg (fun _a => a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul a (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                      (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                                Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                  (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf δ rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg (fun _a => δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul δ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                      (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                                Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                  (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul δ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                        (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 1))))))
                                Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero a (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero δ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_le_of_neg (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le haδ)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a_1))))))
        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.div_congr
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf a rfl
                (Eq.mpr
                  (id (congrArg (fun _a => a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a) (Eq.symm rfl)))
                  (Eq.refl (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf K rfl
                (Eq.mpr
                  (id (congrArg (fun _a => K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a) (Eq.symm rfl)))
                  (Eq.refl (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left a (Nat.rawCast 1)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right K (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                    (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0)))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                  (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0))))
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf δ rfl
                (Eq.mpr
                  (id (congrArg (fun _a => δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a) (Eq.symm rfl)))
                  (Eq.refl (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul δ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                        (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                  Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.div_pf
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf'
                (Eq.refl (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹) rfl
                (Eq.mpr
                  (id
                    (congrArg
                      (fun _a =>
                        (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                            Nat.rawCast 1 =
                          (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                      (Eq.symm rfl)))
                  (Eq.refl
                    ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                      Nat.rawCast 1))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left a (Nat.rawCast 1)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left K (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                        (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1))))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                    (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                        (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                          ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                            Nat.rawCast 1)) +
                      0)))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                  ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                      Nat.rawCast 1 +
                    0))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                  (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                      (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                        ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                          Nat.rawCast 1)) +
                    0)))))
          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.div_congr
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf K rfl
                (Eq.mpr
                  (id (congrArg (fun _a => K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a) (Eq.symm rfl)))
                  (Eq.refl (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf δ rfl
                  (Eq.mpr
                    (id (congrArg (fun _a => δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a) (Eq.symm rfl)))
                    (Eq.refl (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul δ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                          (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                    Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.div_pf
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf'
                  (Eq.refl (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹) rfl
                  (Eq.mpr
                    (id
                      (congrArg
                        (fun _a =>
                          (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                              Nat.rawCast 1 =
                            (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                        (Eq.symm rfl)))
                    (Eq.refl
                      ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                        Nat.rawCast 1))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left K (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                        (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                      (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                          ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                            Nat.rawCast 1) +
                        0)))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                    ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                        Nat.rawCast 1 +
                      0))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                    (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                        ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                          Nat.rawCast 1) +
                      0)))))
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf a rfl
              (Eq.mpr
                (id (congrArg (fun _a => a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a) (Eq.symm rfl)))
                (Eq.refl (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right a (Nat.rawCast 1)
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left K (Nat.rawCast 1)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left
                      (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1))))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero
                  (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                    ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                      Nat.rawCast 1)))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                  (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                      (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                        ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                          Nat.rawCast 1)) +
                    0)))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                    (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                      ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                        Nat.rawCast 1)) +
                  0))))))
      le_rfl)
theorem Luce.BernoulliProcess.integral_laplace_error_of_atom_bound.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
  {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (X : Luce.BernoulliProcess μ) (g : ℕ → ℝ),
  (∀ (k : ℕ), 0 ≤ g k) →
    ∀ (N : ℕ) {δ K lam : ℝ} {a : Ω → ℝ},
      δ < 1 →
        0 ≤ lam →
          MeasureTheory.Integrable a μ →
            (∀ (ω : Ω), 0 ≤ a ω) →
              (∀ (ω : Ω), a ω ≤ δ) →
                (∀ (k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), X.probability k ω ≤ a ω) →
                  (∀ (ω : Ω), ∑ k ∈ Finset.range N, X.probability k ω ≤ K) →
                    |∫ (ω : Ω), Real.exp (-∑ k ∈ Finset.range N, g k * X.observation k ω) ∂μ - Real.exp (-lam)| ≤
                      Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) *
                        (K / (1 - δ) * ∫ (ω : Ω), a ω ∂μ + ∫ (ω : Ω), |X.laplaceCompensator g N ω - lam| ∂μ) :=
fun {Ω} {mΩ} {μ} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] X g hg N {δ K lam} {a} hδ hlam haint ha0 haδ hp hK =>
  have hpδ := fun k ω => LE.le.trans (hp k ω) (haδ ω);
  have hA := Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceProduct_integrable X hδ g hg hpδ N;
  have hL := Luce.BernoulliProcess.likelihood_integrable X hδ g hg hpδ N;
  have hS := Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceCompensator_integrable X g N;
  have hbound :=
    Luce.likelihood_integral_error hL hA (Luce.BernoulliProcess.integral_likelihood X hδ g hg hpδ N)
      (MeasureTheory.ae_of_all μ fun ω =>
        (Luce.BernoulliProcess.likelihood_bounds_of_sum_le X hδ g hg hpδ N ω (hK ω)).left)
      (MeasureTheory.ae_of_all μ fun ω =>
        (Luce.BernoulliProcess.likelihood_bounds_of_sum_le X hδ g hg hpδ N ω (hK ω)).right);
  have heq := funext fun ω => Luce.BernoulliProcess.likelihood_mul_laplaceProduct X hδ g hg hpδ N ω;
  LE.le.trans
    (Eq.mp
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          |MeasureTheory.integral μ _a - Real.exp (-lam)| ≤
            Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) * ∫ (ω : Ω), |X.laplaceProduct g N ω - Real.exp (-lam)| ∂μ)
        heq)
      hbound)
    (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left
      (Trans.trans
        (MeasureTheory.integral_mono_ae
          (MeasureTheory.Integrable.abs
            (MeasureTheory.Integrable.sub hA (MeasureTheory.integrable_const (Real.exp (-lam)))))
          (MeasureTheory.Integrable.add (MeasureTheory.Integrable.const_mul haint (K / (1 - δ)))
            (MeasureTheory.Integrable.abs (MeasureTheory.Integrable.sub hS (MeasureTheory.integrable_const lam))))
          (MeasureTheory.ae_of_all μ fun ω =>
            Luce.product_poisson_error_of_atom_bound (Finset.range N)
              (fun k => X.probability k ω * (1 - Real.exp (-g k)))
              (fun k a =>
                mul_nonneg (X.probability_nonneg k ω)
                  (sub_nonneg.mpr (Real.exp_le_one_iff.mpr (neg_nonpos.mpr (hg k)))))
              (fun k a_1 =>
                LE.le.trans
                  (mul_le_of_le_one_right (X.probability_nonneg k ω)
                    (le_of_not_gt fun a =>
                      Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
                        (Eq.mp
                          (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.exp (-g k)) rfl
                                      (Eq.mpr
                                        (id
                                          (congrArg
                                            (fun _a =>
                                              Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                                Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                            (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                        (Eq.refl (Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (-g k)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                              (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                          (Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                          (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (-g k)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                                (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 1))))))
                                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                          (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                        (Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.exp (-g k)) rfl
                                    (Eq.mpr
                                      (id
                                        (congrArg
                                          (fun _a =>
                                            Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                              Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                          (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                      (Eq.refl (Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (-g k)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                            (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                                      Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                      (Real.exp (-g k) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (Real.exp (-g k)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                        (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0)))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_neg (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt (Real.exp_pos (-g k)))))))
                  (hp k ω))
              (ha0 ω) (haδ ω) hδ (LE.le.trans (Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceCompensator_bounds X g hg N ω).right (hK ω))
              hlam))
        (have hr := MeasureTheory.Integrable.abs (MeasureTheory.Integrable.sub hS (MeasureTheory.integrable_const lam));
        Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a => _a = K / (1 - δ) * ∫ (ω : Ω), a ω ∂μ + ∫ (ω : Ω), |X.laplaceCompensator g N ω - lam| ∂μ)
              (MeasureTheory.integral_add (MeasureTheory.Integrable.const_mul haint (K / (1 - δ))) hr)))
          (Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg
                (fun _a =>
                  _a + ∫ (a : Ω), |X.laplaceCompensator g N a - lam| ∂μ =
                    K / (1 - δ) * ∫ (ω : Ω), a ω ∂μ + ∫ (ω : Ω), |X.laplaceCompensator g N ω - lam| ∂μ)
                (MeasureTheory.integral_const_mul (K / (1 - δ)) a)))
            (Eq.refl (K / (1 - δ) * ∫ (a_1 : Ω), a a_1 ∂μ + ∫ (a : Ω), |X.laplaceCompensator g N a - lam| ∂μ)))))
      (LT.lt.le (Real.exp_pos (K / (1 - δ)))))
def Luce.ConvergesInProbability.{u_1} : {Ω : ℕ → Type u_1} →
  [inst : (n : ℕ) → MeasurableSpace (Ω n)] → ((n : ℕ) → MeasureTheory.Measure (Ω n)) → ((n : ℕ) → Ω n → ℝ) → ℝ → Prop :=
fun {Ω} [(n : ℕ) → MeasurableSpace (Ω n)] μ X c =>
  ∀ (ε : ℝ), 0 < ε → Filter.Tendsto (fun n => (μ n).real {ω | ε < |X n ω - c|}) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
theorem Luce.ConvergesInProbability.congr_off.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : ℕ → Type u_1} [inst : (n : ℕ) → MeasurableSpace (Ω n)]
  {μ : (n : ℕ) → MeasureTheory.Measure (Ω n)} [∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure (μ n)]
  {X Y : (n : ℕ) → Ω n → ℝ} {c : ℝ},
  Luce.ConvergesInProbability μ Y c →
    ∀ (bad : (n : ℕ) → Set (Ω n)),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n => (μ n).real (bad n)) Filter.atTop (nhds 0) →
        (∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ ω ∉ bad n, X n ω = Y n ω) → Luce.ConvergesInProbability μ X c :=
fun {Ω} [(n : ℕ) → MeasurableSpace (Ω n)] {μ} [∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure (μ n)] {X Y} {c} hY bad
    hbad heq ε hε =>
  have ht := Filter.Tendsto.add (hY ε hε) hbad;
  squeeze_zero (fun x => MeasureTheory.measureReal_nonneg)
    (fun n =>
      Trans.trans
        (MeasureTheory.measureReal_mono
          (fun ⦃ω⦄ hω =>
            if hb : ω ∈ bad n then Or.inr hb
            else Or.inl (id (fun hω => id (Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => ε < |_a - c|) (heq n ω hb)) hω)) hω))
          (MeasureTheory.measure_ne_top (μ n) ({ω | ε < |Y n ω - c|} ∪ bad n)))
        (MeasureTheory.measureReal_union_le {ω | ε < |Y n ω - c|} (bad n)))
    (Eq.mp
      (congrArg (Filter.Tendsto (fun x => (μ x).real {ω | ε < |Y x ω - c|} + (μ x).real (bad x)) Filter.atTop)
        (congrArg nhds (add_zero 0)))
      ht)
theorem Luce.ConvergesInProbability.integral_abs_tendsto.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : ℕ → Type u_1}
  [inst : (n : ℕ) → MeasurableSpace (Ω n)] {μ : (n : ℕ) → MeasureTheory.Measure (Ω n)}
  [∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure (μ n)] {X : (n : ℕ) → Ω n → ℝ} {c : ℝ},
  Luce.ConvergesInProbability μ X c →
    (∀ (n : ℕ), Measurable (X n)) →
      ∀ {B : ℝ},
        (∀ (n : ℕ) (ω : Ω n), |X n ω - c| ≤ B) →
          Filter.Tendsto (fun n => ∫ (ω : Ω n), |X n ω - c| ∂μ n) Filter.atTop (nhds 0) :=
fun {Ω} [(n : ℕ) → MeasurableSpace (Ω n)] {μ} [∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure (μ n)] {X} {c} hX hXmeas
    {B} hbound =>
  Luce.tendsto_integral_abs_of_bounded_probability μ X hXmeas hbound hX
theorem Luce.BernoulliProcess.capped_laplace_tendsto.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
  {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω} [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (X : ℕ → Luce.BernoulliProcess μ) (g : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)
  (N : ℕ → ℕ) {δ K lam : ℝ},
  δ < 1 →
    0 ≤ lam →
      (∀ (n k : ℕ), 0 ≤ g n k) →
        ∀ (a : ℕ → Ω → ℝ),
          (∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.Integrable (a n) μ) →
            (∀ (n : ℕ) (ω : Ω), 0 ≤ a n ω) →
              (∀ (n : ℕ) (ω : Ω), a n ω ≤ δ) →
                (∀ (n k : ℕ) (ω : Ω), (X n).probability k ω ≤ a n ω) →
                  (∀ (n : ℕ) (ω : Ω), ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N n), (X n).probability k ω ≤ K) →
                    (MeasureTheory.TendstoInMeasure μ a Filter.atTop fun x => 0) →
                      (MeasureTheory.TendstoInMeasure μ (fun n => (X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n)) Filter.atTop
                          fun x => lam) →
                        Filter.Tendsto
                          (fun n => ∫ (ω : Ω), Real.exp (-∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N n), g n k * (X n).observation k ω) ∂μ)
                          Filter.atTop (nhds (Real.exp (-lam))) :=
fun {Ω} {mΩ} {μ} [⋯] X g N {δ K lam} hδ hlam hg a haint ha0 haδ hp hK hatendsto hcomp =>
  have hta :=
    have h :=
      Luce.tendsto_integral_abs_of_bounded_inMeasure (fun n => MeasureTheory.Integrable.aestronglyMeasurable (haint n))
        (fun n =>
          MeasureTheory.ae_of_all μ fun ω =>
            Eq.mpr (id (congrFun' (congrArg LE.le (abs_of_nonneg (ha0 n ω))) δ)) (haδ n ω))
        hatendsto;
    Eq.mp
      (congrFun'
        (congrFun'
          (congrArg Filter.Tendsto
            (funext fun n =>
              congrArg (MeasureTheory.integral μ)
                (funext fun ω =>
                  Eq.trans (congrArg abs (sub_zero (a n ω)))
                    ((fun x_0 x_1 => (fun x_0 x_1 => abs_of_nonneg (ha0 x_0 x_1)) x_0 x_1) n ω))))
          Filter.atTop)
        (nhds 0))
      h;
  have htc :=
    Luce.tendsto_integral_abs_of_bounded_inMeasure
      (fun n =>
        MeasureTheory.Integrable.aestronglyMeasurable
          (Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceCompensator_integrable (X n) (g n) (N n)))
      (fun n =>
        MeasureTheory.ae_of_all μ fun ω =>
          Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ K)
                (abs_of_nonneg (Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceCompensator_bounds (X n) (g n) (hg n) (N n) ω).left)))
            (LE.le.trans (Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceCompensator_bounds (X n) (g n) (hg n) (N n) ω).right (hK n ω)))
      hcomp;
  let error := fun n =>
    Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) *
      (K / (1 - δ) * ∫ (ω : Ω), a n ω ∂μ + ∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ);
  have he :=
    id
      (Eq.mp
        (congrArg
          (Filter.Tendsto
            (fun x =>
              Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) *
                (K / (1 - δ) * ∫ (ω : Ω), a x ω ∂μ + ∫ (ω : Ω), |(X x).laplaceCompensator (g x) (N x) ω - lam| ∂μ))
            Filter.atTop)
          (congrArg nhds
            (Eq.trans
              (congrArg (HMul.hMul (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))))
                (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg HAdd.hAdd (mul_zero (K / (1 - δ)))) 0) (add_zero 0)))
              (mul_zero (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)))))))
        (Filter.Tendsto.const_mul (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)))
          (Filter.Tendsto.add (Filter.Tendsto.const_mul (K / (1 - δ)) hta) htc)));
  tendsto_of_tendsto_of_tendsto_of_le_of_le
    (Eq.mp
      (congrArg (Filter.Tendsto (fun x => Real.exp (-lam) - error x) Filter.atTop)
        (congrArg nhds (sub_zero (Real.exp (-lam)))))
      (Filter.Tendsto.sub tendsto_const_nhds he))
    (Eq.mp
      (congrArg (Filter.Tendsto (fun x => Real.exp (-lam) + error x) Filter.atTop)
        (congrArg nhds (add_zero (Real.exp (-lam)))))
      (Filter.Tendsto.add tendsto_const_nhds he))
    (fun n =>
      have h :=
        (abs_le.mp
            (Luce.BernoulliProcess.integral_laplace_error_of_atom_bound (X n) (g n) (hg n) (N n) hδ hlam (haint n)
              (ha0 n) (haδ n) (hp n) (hK n))).left;
      id
        (id
          (le_of_not_gt fun a_1 =>
            Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
              (Eq.mp
                (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)

                                        ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^
                                            Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          ((∫ (ω : Ω), a n ω ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)) +
                                      0))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf
                                  (∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) rfl
                                  (Eq.mpr
                                    (id
                                      (congrArg
                                        (fun _a =>
                                          (∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) ^
                                                Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                              Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                            (∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) ^
                                                Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                              _a)
                                        (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                    (Eq.refl
                                      ((∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                      (∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1))))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 1))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                      ((∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                        0)))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                    ((∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                      0))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                    ((∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                      0))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                                (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    ((∫ (ω : Ω), a n ω ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                  ((∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                    0))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right K (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                      (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹
                                      (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (∫ (ω : Ω), a n ω ∂μ) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1)))))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                      (∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1)))))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero
                                    (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))

                                        ((∫ (ω : Ω), a n ω ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))

                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))) (Nat.rawCast 1)

                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul
                                    (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹
                                    (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (∫ (ω : Ω), a n ω ∂μ) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                          (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul
                                    (∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                        (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                                Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                              (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    ((∫ (ω : Ω), a n ω ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                                (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  ((∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                  (Real.exp (-lam) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul K (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul
                                  (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (∫ (ω : Ω), a n ω ∂μ) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                          (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 1)))))))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul
                                  (∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                        (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 1)))))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.exp (-lam)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                      (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                                Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                            (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                              (K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ ^
                                    Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  ((∫ (ω : Ω), a n ω ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                              (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                ((∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  Nat.rawCast 1))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                                ((∫ (ω : Ω), Real.exp (-∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N n), g n k * (X n).observation k ω) ∂μ) ^
                                    Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                  (Real.exp (-lam) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero K (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero
                              (Nat.rawCast 1 + (δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))⁻¹ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (∫ (ω : Ω), a n ω ∂μ) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                    (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero
                              (∫ (ω : Ω), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero
                              (∫ (ω : Ω), Real.exp (-∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N n), g n k * (X n).observation k ω) ∂μ)
                              (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (Real.exp (-lam)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat ⋯))
                              ⋯)))))
                    ⋯))
                ⋯))))
    ⋯
theorem Luce.BernoulliProcess.capped_laplace_tendsto_rows.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : ℕ → Type u_1}
  [inst : (n : ℕ) → MeasurableSpace (Ω n)] (μ : (n : ℕ) → MeasureTheory.Measure (Ω n))
  [∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure (μ n)] (X : (n : ℕ) → Luce.BernoulliProcess (μ n)) (g : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)
  (N : ℕ → ℕ) {δ K lam : ℝ},
  δ < 1 →
    0 ≤ lam →
      (∀ (n k : ℕ), 0 ≤ g n k) →
        ∀ (a : (n : ℕ) → Ω n → ℝ),
          (∀ (n : ℕ), Measurable (a n)) →
            (∀ (n : ℕ) (ω : Ω n), 0 ≤ a n ω) →
              (∀ (n : ℕ) (ω : Ω n), a n ω ≤ δ) →
                (∀ (n k : ℕ) (ω : Ω n), (X n).probability k ω ≤ a n ω) →
                  (∀ (n : ℕ) (ω : Ω n), ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N n), (X n).probability k ω ≤ K) →
                    Luce.ConvergesInProbability μ a 0 →
                      Luce.ConvergesInProbability μ (fun n => (X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n)) lam →
                        Filter.Tendsto
                          (fun n =>
                            ∫ (ω : Ω n), Real.exp (-∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N n), g n k * (X n).observation k ω) ∂μ n)
                          Filter.atTop (nhds (Real.exp (-lam))) :=
fun {Ω} [⋯] μ [⋯] X g N {δ K lam} hδ hlam hg a hameas ha0 haδ hp hK hatendsto hcomp =>
  have haint := fun n =>
    ⟨Measurable.aestronglyMeasurable (hameas n),
      MeasureTheory.HasFiniteIntegral.of_mem_Icc 0 δ (MeasureTheory.ae_of_all (μ n) fun ω => ⟨ha0 n ω, haδ n ω⟩)⟩;
  have hta :=
    have h :=
      Luce.ConvergesInProbability.integral_abs_tendsto hatendsto hameas fun n ω =>
        Eq.mpr
          (id (congrFun' (congrArg LE.le (Eq.trans (congrArg abs (sub_zero (a n ω))) (abs_of_nonneg (ha0 n ω)))) δ))
          (haδ n ω);
    Eq.mp
      (congrFun'
        (congrFun'
          (congrArg Filter.Tendsto
            (funext fun n =>
              congrArg (MeasureTheory.integral (μ n))
                (funext fun ω =>
                  Eq.trans (congrArg abs (sub_zero (a n ω)))
                    ((fun x_0 x_1 => (fun x_0 x_1 => abs_of_nonneg (ha0 x_0 x_1)) x_0 x_1) n ω))))
          Filter.atTop)
        (nhds 0))
      h;
  have hSmeas := fun n =>
    Finset.measurable_sum (Finset.range (N n)) fun k a =>
      Measurable.mul_const
        (MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable.measurable
          (MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable.mono ((X n).predictable k)
            (MeasureTheory.Filtration.le (X n).filtration k)))
        (1 - Real.exp (-g n k));
  have htc :=
    Luce.ConvergesInProbability.integral_abs_tendsto hcomp hSmeas fun n ω =>
      have hb := Luce.BernoulliProcess.laplaceCompensator_bounds (X n) (g n) (hg n) (N n) ω;
      Trans.trans (abs_sub ((X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω) lam)
        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a + |lam| ≤ K + |lam|) (abs_of_nonneg hb.left)))
          (add_le_add (LE.le.trans hb.right (hK n ω)) le_rfl));
  let error := fun n =>
    Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) *
      (K / (1 - δ) * ∫ (ω : Ω n), a n ω ∂μ n + ∫ (ω : Ω n), |(X n).laplaceCompensator (g n) (N n) ω - lam| ∂μ n);
  have he :=
    id
      (Eq.mp
        (congrArg
          (Filter.Tendsto
            (fun x =>
              Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) *
                (K / (1 - δ) * ∫ (ω : Ω x), a x ω ∂μ x +
                  ∫ (ω : Ω x), |(X x).laplaceCompensator (g x) (N x) ω - lam| ∂μ x))
            Filter.atTop)
          (congrArg nhds
            (Eq.trans
              (congrArg (HMul.hMul (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))))
                (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg HAdd.hAdd (mul_zero (K / (1 - δ)))) 0) (add_zero 0)))
              (mul_zero (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)))))))
        (Filter.Tendsto.const_mul (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)))
          (Filter.Tendsto.add (Filter.Tendsto.const_mul (K / (1 - δ)) hta) htc)));
  tendsto_of_tendsto_of_tendsto_of_le_of_le
    (Eq.mp
      (congrArg (Filter.Tendsto (fun x => Real.exp (-lam) - error x) Filter.atTop)
        (congrArg nhds (sub_zero (Real.exp (-lam)))))
      (Filter.Tendsto.sub tendsto_const_nhds he))
    (Eq.mp
      (congrArg (Filter.Tendsto (fun x => Real.exp (-lam) + error x) Filter.atTop)
        (congrArg nhds (add_zero (Real.exp (-lam)))))
      (Filter.Tendsto.add tendsto_const_nhds he))
    (fun n =>
      have h :=
        (abs_le.mp
            (Luce.BernoulliProcess.integral_laplace_error_of_atom_bound (X n) (g n) (hg n) (N n) hδ hlam (haint n)
              (ha0 n) (haδ n) (hp n) (hK n))).left;
      id
        (le_of_not_gt fun a_1 =>
          Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
            (Eq.mp
              (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))) rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg
                                    (fun _a =>
                                      Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                        Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ))) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                      (Eq.refl 1))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 1))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                  (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                (Real.exp (K / (1 - δ)) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.div_congr
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf K rfl
                                    (Eq.mpr
                                      (id
